\lxSVG@closescope
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}

(69)

Using the operator definition of the fragmentation function as pointed out in
A
, along with the SCET2 Feynman rules, as presented in Section
2
, in the complex mass scheme, we get:

\displaystyle D_{0,th}=\dfrac{z}{2}y_{t}^{2}\int\dfrac{d^{4}k}{(2\pi)^{4}}

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left\{\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\left(p^{+}+k^{+}\right)\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\left(\dfrac{\not{k}_{\perp}}{k^{+}}-\dfrac{\not{p}_{\perp}+\not{k}_{\perp}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}-\dfrac{\sqrt{M^{2}_{t}-iM_{t}\Gamma_{t}}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\right)\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}k^{+}\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\left(\dfrac{\not{k}_{\perp}}{k^{+}}-\dfrac{\not{p}_{\perp}+\not{k}_{\perp}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}+\dfrac{\sqrt{M^{2}_{t}+iM_{t}\Gamma_{t}}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\right)\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\left(p^{+}+k^{+}\right)\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\right\}2\pi\delta\left(k^{2}\right)\dfrac{\delta\left(Q-p^{+}-k^{+}\right)}{\left((p+k)^{2}-M_{t}^{2}\right)^{2}+M_{t}^{2}\Gamma^{2}},

(70)

where the “0” subscript denotes that this is the bare fragmentation function. We follow the steps outlined in
3.1
, performing the calculation in the reference frame, discussed in
A
where daughter’s perp momentum is zero (
p_{\perp}=0
):

\displaystyle D_{0,th}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{z}{2}y_{t}^{2}\int\dfrac{d^{4}k}{(2\pi)^{4}}

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left\{\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\left(p^{+}+k^{+}\right)\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\left(\dfrac{p^{+}\not{k}_{\perp}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})k^{+}}-\dfrac{\sqrt{M^{2}_{t}-iM_{t}\Gamma_{t}}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\right)\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}k^{+}\left(\dfrac{p^{+}\not{k}_{\perp}}{(p^{+}+k^{+})k^{+}}+\dfrac{\sqrt{M^{2}_{t}+iM_{t}\Gamma_{t}}}{p^{+}+k^{+}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\left(p^{+}+k^{+}\right)\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\right\}2\pi\delta\left(k^{2}\right)\dfrac{\delta\left(Q-p^{+}-k^{+}\right)}{\left((p+k)^{+}(p+k)^{-}-k_{\perp}^{2}-M_{t}^{2}\right)^{2}+M_{t}^{2}\Gamma^{2}}.

(71)

The next step is to calculate the trace and simplify the Dirac structure:

\displaystyle D_{0,th}=\dfrac{z}{2}y_{t}^{2}\int\dfrac{d^{4}k}{(2\pi)^{3}}\left(p^{+}+k^{+}\right)^{2}k^{+}\left(\dfrac{k_{\perp}^{2}p_{+}^{2}+M_{t}^{2}k_{+}^{2}-M_{t}\Gamma k_{+}^{2}}{k^{2}_{+}(p_{+}+k_{+})^{2}}\right)\dfrac{\delta\left(k^{2}\right)\delta\left(Q-p^{+}-k^{+}\right)}{\left((p+k)^{+}(p+k)^{-}-k_{\perp}^{2}-M_{t}^{2}\right)^{2}+M_{t}^{2}\Gamma^{2}}.

(72)

We integrate
k^{-}
with
k
on-shell and also
p^{-}=0
due to
p
on-shellness since we are in the daughter’s reference frame:

\displaystyle D_{0,th}=\dfrac{z}{2}\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}\int\dfrac{d^{2}k_{\perp}dk^{+}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\left(k_{\perp}^{2}\dfrac{p_{+}^{2}}{k^{2}_{+}}+M_{t}^{2}\right)\dfrac{\delta\left(Q-p^{+}-k^{+}\right)}{\left((p+k)^{+}\dfrac{k_{\perp}^{2}}{k^{+}}-k_{\perp}^{2}-M_{t}^{2}\right)^{2}+M_{t}^{2}\Gamma^{2}}+\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{2}_{Y}\right).

(73)

Here in this step we focused only on the terms that are leading order in
\alpha_{y}
, any terms of
\mathcal{O}\left(\alpha^{2}_{y}\right)
are subsequently dropped. Defining
z\equiv\dfrac{p^{+}}{Q}
and integrating over
k^{+}
with the remaining delta function,
\delta\left(Q-k^{+}-p^{+}\right)
:

\displaystyle D_{0,th}=\dfrac{z}{2}\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}\int\dfrac{d^{2}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\left(k_{\perp}^{2}\dfrac{z^{2}}{(1-z)^{2}}+M_{t}^{2}\right)\dfrac{\dfrac{(1-z)^{2}}{z^{2}}}{\left(k_{\perp}^{2}-M_{t}^{2}\dfrac{1-z}{z}\right)^{2}+M_{t}^{2}\Gamma^{2}\dfrac{(1-z)^{2}}{z^{2}}}.

(74)

Regularizing the UV divergence through Dimensional Regularization in
d=2-2\varepsilon
:

\displaystyle D_{0,th}=\dfrac{z}{2}\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}\mu^{2\varepsilon}\int\dfrac{d^{d}k_{\perp}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\dfrac{\left(k_{\perp}^{2}+M_{t}^{2}\dfrac{(1-z)^{2}}{z^{2}}\right)}{\left(k_{\perp}^{2}-M_{t}^{2}\dfrac{1-z}{z}\right)^{2}+M_{t}^{2}\Gamma^{2}\dfrac{(1-z)^{2}}{z^{2}}},

(75)

Integrating
k_{\perp}
from
[0,\infty)
and expanding at
\varepsilon=0
:

\displaystyle D_{0,th}=\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{4\pi}\left(z\dfrac{1}{\varepsilon}+z\log\left(\dfrac{\mu^{2}}{M_{t}^{2}\dfrac{1-z}{z}\sqrt{1+\dfrac{\Gamma^{2}}{M_{t}^{2}}}}\right)-\dfrac{M_{t}}{\Gamma_{t}}\arctan\left(\dfrac{\Gamma_{t}}{M_{t}}\right)+\pi\dfrac{M_{t}}{\Gamma_{t}}\right).

(76)

We note that the
M_{t}^{2}
term in the numerator of Eq.
75
contributes the
z\log[z/(1-z)]
in
D_{0,th}
that one can spot in Eq.
76
. This is precisely the ultracollinear contribution. It relies on keeping finite
M_{t}
in the calculation, but it persists after the fact even if we take
M_{t}\rightarrow 0
.
The final form of the fragmentation function is given after keeping the LO terms of
\dfrac{\Gamma_{t}}{M_{t}}\rightarrow 0
(to
\mathcal{O}(\alpha_{y})
,
\Gamma_{t}=\alpha_{y}M_{t}/4
) and subtracting the
\dfrac{1}{\varepsilon}
term. Therefore, we have the renormalized fragmentation function,
D_{th}
:

\displaystyle\boxed{D_{th}=\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{4\pi}\left(z\log\left(\dfrac{\mu^{2}}{M_{t}^{2}}\right)-z\log\left(\dfrac{1-z}{z}\right)-1+\pi\dfrac{M_{t}}{\Gamma_{t}}\right)}

(77)

C.2
Calculation of
D_{hh}
in Intermediate Theory with
M_{t}\neq 0

The diagram for
D_{hh}
calculation is the following:

(78)

We follow the steps outlined in
3.1
and we calculate the residue of the expression represented by this graph:

\displaystyle\text{Res}\left[\Sigma\left(p\right)\right]=\lim_{p^{2}\rightarrow 0}\dfrac{d}{dp^{2}}\left\{\left(p^{2}\right)^{2}\cdot\dfrac{1}{p^{2}}\Sigma\left(p\right)\cdot\dfrac{1}{p^{2}}\right\}=\lim_{p^{-}\rightarrow 0}\dfrac{dp^{-}}{dp^{2}}\dfrac{d}{dp^{-}}\Sigma\left(p\right)=\lim_{p^{-}\rightarrow 0}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\dfrac{d}{dp^{-}}\Sigma\left(p\right),

(79)

where
\Sigma\left(p\right)
is given by:

\displaystyle\Sigma\left(p\right)=(-1)y_{t}^{2}\int\dfrac{d^{4}k}{(2\pi)^{4}}

\displaystyle\text{Tr}\left\{\left(\dfrac{-\not{k}_{\perp}}{p^{+}-k^{+}}-\dfrac{\not{k}_{\perp}}{k^{+}}-\dfrac{M_{t}}{k^{+}}\right)\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\left(\dfrac{-\not{k}_{\perp}}{p^{+}-k^{+}}-\dfrac{\not{k}_{\perp}}{k^{+}}+\dfrac{M_{t}}{k^{+}}\right)\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\dfrac{k^{+}}{k^{+}k^{-}-k^{2}_{\perp}-M_{t}^{2}}\left(\dfrac{(p-k)^{+}}{(p-k)^{+}(p-k)^{-}-k^{2}_{\perp}}\right),

(80)

and the
(-1)
comes from the fermion loop factor. The derivative gives:

\displaystyle\text{Res}\left[\Sigma\left(p\right)\right]=\lim_{p^{-}\rightarrow 0}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\dfrac{d}{dp^{-}}\Sigma\left(p\right)

\displaystyle=-y_{t}^{2}2\int\dfrac{d^{4}k}{(2\pi)^{4}}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\left(\dfrac{p_{+}^{2}k^{2}_{\perp}}{k^{+}(p^{+}-k^{+})}-\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}(p^{+}-k^{+})}{k^{+}}\right)

\displaystyle\dfrac{(k-p)^{+}}{k^{+}k^{-}-k^{2}_{\perp}-M^{2}_{t}}\left(\dfrac{1}{(p-k)^{+}(-k^{-})-k^{2}_{\perp}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=-y_{t}^{2}2\int\dfrac{d^{4}k}{(2\pi)^{4}}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\left(\dfrac{p_{+}^{2}k^{2}_{\perp}}{k^{+}(p^{+}-k^{+})}-\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}(p^{+}-k^{+})}{k^{+}}\right)

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{k^{+}k^{-}-k^{2}_{\perp}-M^{2}_{t}}\dfrac{1}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}\left(\dfrac{1}{k^{-}-\dfrac{k^{2}_{\perp}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}}\right)^{2}.

(81)

Above, the trace is
\text{Tr}\left\{\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\dfrac{\not{\bar{n}}}{2}\dfrac{\not{n}}{2}\right\}=2
. Using the residue theorem, the integral over
k^{-}
can be calculated by expanding the integrand around the pole,
k^{-}=(k_{\perp}^{2}+M_{t}^{2})/k^{+}
. The integral is nonzero if
p^{+}>k^{+}>0
. Thus,

\displaystyle\text{Res}\left[\Sigma\left(p\right)\right]=

\displaystyle-\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}2\int\dfrac{d^{2}k_{\perp}dk^{+}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\left(\dfrac{p_{+}^{2}k^{2}_{\perp}}{k^{+}(p^{+}-k^{+})}-\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}(p^{+}-k^{+})}{k^{+}}\right)\dfrac{1}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}\dfrac{1}{k^{+}}\left(\dfrac{1}{\dfrac{k^{2}_{\perp}}{k^{+}}+\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}}{k^{+}}-\dfrac{k^{2}_{\perp}}{(k^{+}-p^{+})}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}2\int\dfrac{d^{2}k_{\perp}dk^{+}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\left(\dfrac{p_{+}^{2}k^{2}_{\perp}}{k^{+}(p^{+}-k^{+})}-\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}(p^{+}-k^{+})}{k^{+}}\right)\dfrac{1}{(k^{+}-p^{+})k^{+}}\left(\dfrac{1}{\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}}{k^{+}}-\dfrac{k^{2}_{\perp}p^{+}}{k^{+}(k^{+}-p^{+})}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=-\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}2\int\dfrac{d^{2}k_{\perp}dk^{+}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\left(-k^{2}_{\perp}+M_{t}^{2}\dfrac{(k^{+}-p^{+})^{2}}{(p^{+})^{2}}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{k^{2}_{\perp}-\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}(k^{+}-p^{+})}{p^{+}}}\right)^{2}.

(82)

The
k_{\perp}
in the numerator of the expression above is not Euclidean. Thus we need to rewrite the expression as follows:

\displaystyle\text{Res}\left[\Sigma\left(p\right)\right]=-\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}2\int\dfrac{d^{2}k_{\perp}dk^{+}}{(2\pi)^{3}}\dfrac{1}{p^{+}}\left(k^{2}_{\perp}+M_{t}^{2}\dfrac{(k^{+}-p^{+})^{2}}{(p^{+})^{2}}\right)\left(\dfrac{1}{k^{2}_{\perp}-\dfrac{M_{t}^{2}(k^{+}-p^{+})}{p^{+}}}\right)^{2}.

(83)

Integrating first over
k_{\perp}
on
\left[0,\infty\right)
using Dimensional Regularization:

\displaystyle\text{Res}\left[\Sigma\left(p\right)\right]=-\dfrac{y_{t}^{2}}{2}2\int_{0}^{p^{+}}\dfrac{dk^{+}}{8(\pi)^{2}}\frac{\pi(k^{+}-p^{+})\mu^{2\epsilon}\csc(\pi\epsilon)(k^{+}\epsilon-2p^{+})\left(\frac{p^{+}}{M_{t}^{2}(p^{+}-k^{+})}\right)^{\epsilon}}{(p^{+})^{2}(\epsilon-2)}.

(84)

Integrating over
k^{+}
and expanding to
\mathcal{O}(\epsilon^{0})
, we get the 1-loop contribution as:

\displaystyle\text{Res}\left[\Sigma\left(p\right)\right]=\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{2\pi}\left[-\log\left(\dfrac{\mu^{2}}{M_{t}^{2}}\right)-\dfrac{1}{\epsilon}-\dfrac{1}{2}\right].

(85)

Working toward the fragmentation function, we restore the
z
dependence, through a
\delta(1-z)
as we have in the self-fragmentation:

\displaystyle D_{0,hh}=\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{2\pi}\left[-\log\left(\dfrac{\mu^{2}}{M_{t}^{2}}\right)-\dfrac{1}{\epsilon}-\dfrac{1}{2}\right]\delta(1-z).

(86)

The final form of the renormalized fragmentation function, after subtracting the
\dfrac{1}{\varepsilon}
, is

\displaystyle\boxed{D_{hh}(z)=\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{2\pi}\left[-\log\left(\dfrac{\mu^{2}}{M_{t}^{2}}\right)-\dfrac{1}{2}\right]\delta\left(1-z\right).}

(87)

The -1/2 term is the ultracollinear contribution in this case.

Appendix D
Mellin Space Anomalous Dimensions

As we saw in
4.2
, performing the running requires us to calculate the anomalous dimension matrix in Mellin space. This can be done at LL and NLL-UC, as dictated in equations
4.2
,
45
, using the matrix elements for fragmentation functions in Mellin space from
3.2
. We list the expressions for anomalous dimension matrix elements at LL and NLL-UC in the tables below:

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{bi}
for
i\in\left\{h,b,t\right\}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{bh}

\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{(s-1)}{s^{2}}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{bb}

-\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{\left(s^{3}+s^{2}+2\right)}{2s^{2}(s+1)}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{bt}

\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{1}{\left(s^{2}+s\right)}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{hi}
for
i\in\left\{h,b,t\right\}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{hh}

-\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{\left(s^{2}+1\right)}{s^{2}}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{hb}

\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{(s-1)}{s^{2}}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{ht}

\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{1}{s}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{ti}
for
i\in\left\{h,b,t\right\}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{th}

\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{1+3s}{4(s^{2}+s)}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{tb}

\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{3+s}{4(s^{2}+s)}

\gamma^{\text{LL}}_{tt}

-\dfrac{\alpha_{y}}{\pi}\dfrac{1}{4}

Table 3
:
Mellin Transforms of the Anomalous Dimension at LL.

for
i\in\left\{h,b,t\right\}

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{bh}

\alpha_{y}^{2}\dfrac{\left(-2(s+1)s^{4}H(s+1)-\left(5s^{4}+10s^{3}+9s^{2}+12s+8\right)sH(s)+2s^{7}-5s^{6}-2s^{5}+35s^{4}+38s^{3}+26s^{2}+32s+16\right)}{8\pi^{2}(s+1)^{3}s^{5}}

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{bb}

-\alpha^{2}_{y}\dfrac{\left(4\left(5s^{4}+12s^{3}+11s^{2}+12s+8\right)sH(s)+27s^{8}+81s^{7}+117s^{6}+51s^{5}-144s^{4}-148s^{3}-48s^{2}-112s-64\right)}{32\pi^{2}s^{5}(s+1)^{3}}

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{bt}

\alpha^{2}_{y}\dfrac{\left(\left(5s^{4}+10s^{3}+9s^{2}+12s+8\right)sH(s)+\left(9s^{6}+13s^{5}-24s^{4}-38s^{3}-22s^{2}-32s-16\right)\right)}{8\pi^{2}s^{4}(s+1)^{3}}

for

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{hh}

-\alpha_{y}^{2}\dfrac{\left(4(s+1)s^{4}H(s+1)+2\left(11s^{4}+22s^{3}+11s^{2}+8s+8\right)sH(s)+11s^{7}+22s^{6}-11s^{5}-78s^{4}-80s^{3}-44s^{2}-48s-32\right)}{16\pi^{2}s^{5}(s+1)^{2}}

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{hb}

\alpha^{2}_{y}\dfrac{\left(-\left(11s^{4}+24s^{3}+13s^{2}+8s+8\right)sH(s)+\left(3s^{6}-6s^{5}+7s^{4}+28s^{3}+8s^{2}+20s+16\right)\right)}{8\pi^{2}s^{5}(s+1)^{2}}

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{ht}

\alpha^{2}_{y}\dfrac{\left(\left(11s^{4}+22s^{3}+11s^{2}+8s+8\right)sH(s)-\left(11s^{5}+37s^{4}+44s^{3}+18s^{2}+24s+16\right)\right)}{8\pi^{2}s^{4}(s+1)^{2}}

for

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{th}

\alpha^{2}_{y}\dfrac{\left(2s\left(3s^{2}+5s+4\right)H(s)+(s+1)\left(8s^{4}H(s+1)-\left(5s^{4}+15s^{3}-4s^{2}+16s+16\right)\right)\right)}{16\pi^{2}s^{4}(s+1)^{2}}

\gamma^{\text{NLL-UC}}_{tb}

\alpha^{2}_{y}\dfrac{\left(4\left(4s^{4}+11s^{3}+12s^{2}+9s+4\right)sH(s)-\left(13s^{6}+49s^{5}+59s^{4}+39s^{3}+68s^{2}+92s+32\right)\right)}{32\pi^{2}s^{4}(s+1)^{3}}

-\alpha^{2}_{y}\dfrac{\left(s\left(3s^{3}+8s^{2}+9s+4\right)H(s)-\left(2s^{4}+7s^{3}+19s^{2}+24s+8\right)\right)}{8\pi^{2}s^{3}(s+1)^{3}}
